---

**Crumbled Sanctuary**
_If you hail Tublik Narwethar after having finished the "Pit of the Lost" quest, you see this:_
Tublik Narwethar says 'Nice to see you again, \_\_\_\_\_. After your recent accomplishments in the Pit of the Lost, I'm more than eager to be working with you once more. This whole time we've been looking at the artifacts that the Muramites have been collecting, but we've been [ignoring something] and I think it's time we looked into it.'
You say, 'Ignoring something?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'Perhaps you've heard of the Altar of Destruction? It is the largest, most visible temple just to the north of here. You can't miss it. If you were to venture just east of it, you would come across a temple that appears destroyed. This temple is called the [Crumbled Sanctuary] of Divine Destruction according to the historical information we've received. I have yet to uncover what its purpose is.'
_Kevren Nalavat has this response if you ask him about it:_
Kevren Nalavat says 'I'm glad you're sticking around to help out with some additional tasks. We can certainly use the help. On the east side of the largest temple to the west, there exists a crumbled temple. As of yet, we're unsure what the cause or reason of the destruction was. I don't have all the information, but Tublik Narwethar does. Seek him out to the south of the Martyrs Passage and find out what you can from him.'
_Ask Tublik about the "Crumbled Sanctuary:_
You say, 'What about the Crumbled Sanctuary?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'Oh yeah, I know all about that thing. It was a temple that was decimated by the Muramites during their initial invasion. We're unsure what caused them to destroy that temple, but not the other three. I need you to go to the temple and [look for clues] as to what the reason may have been for destroying the temple.'
You say, 'I'll look for clues'
Tublik Narwethar says 'I have no idea what the clues may look like, but I'm hoping they'll bring about some insight as to the reason behind the Muramites destroying the temple. Once you've recovered all the clues you can find, please come back to me so I can go over them. If we're lucky, I'll be able to decipher what the real cause was and we can relay that important news to the brotherhood. Off you go, \_\_\_\_\_, and good luck!'

---

!

---

**Clue Collection**
_In the northwestern quadrant of the zone is a giant temple. The "Crumbled Sanctuary" is the small temple to the east, noted for its decaying appearance._
_In this temple are seven ground spawn locations (for your "kodtaz.txt" map file):_
P -510.2631, -3.0832, -399.1795, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
P -530.9882, 120.8708, -402.1943, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
P -495.8418, 185.1053, -407.4394, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
P -532.0091, 183.7077, -407.4096, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
P -542.0750, -75.2371, -378.4202, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
P -511.2050, -92.2363, -378.4423, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
P -521.6580, 237.0889, -407.4115, 0, 127, 0, 1, Frayed\_Flesh\_Scrap
_In-game /locs:_
140, 550, -373 - on top of broken vase
90, 510, -375 - behind other broken vase
75, 540, -376 - right side of the doorway
0, 505, -347 - against the wall by the cave-in
-185, 495, -404 - front left corner of end room
-240, 520, -404 - back middle of end room
-185, 530, -404 - front middle of end room
_Collect these until you have four separate "Frayed Flesh Scrap" (each is lore), then turn in the scraps to Tublik:_
Tublik grunts unhappily at the four pieces. 'I had hoped more work wouldn't be needed to uncover the clues, but it looks like there's a bit more you're going to need to do for me. Take a look at these edges. Do you see how they look really sinewy? That's because they're [made of flesh], probably that of the trusik. No doubt the Muramites have been quite cruel to them now that they are mostly used for slavery.'
_You receive the four items back._
_If you miss this phase Tublik freaks out: 'How is this possible? You couldn't have done this without some kind of trickery! I'm going to alert the appropriate authorities!' - and you lose the item._

---

**A Tailor's (Nasty) Job**
You say, 'These are made of flesh?'
Tublik shakes his head. 'I know it's not very pleasant, but you've got to remember that these Muramites are a new type of scum like nothing else we've ever come across. I think we can salvage some information from this, but it will require you to venture back into areas infested with Muramites. You'll need to [gather some materials] for me so we can sew the flesh back together.'
You say, 'Gather some materials?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'The first thing you'll need is a needle. We can't take any chances with the use of Muramite-altered flesh so you'll need to make your own. To do so, you'll need to find four bone chips and combine them with some of the weird black residue that the Muramites leave behind when they die. You should be able to fashion them together with a sewing kit. The next thing you'll need to do is get some [strands of hair] to keep the flesh together.'
You say, 'What about the strands of hair?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'You'll need to gather four strands of hair from the local hynids. They're not friendly, so be careful when you encounter one. Once you've gotten the strands and made your needle, you're going to need to sew the strands together using the needle. It seems strange I know, but remember that the best solutions are usually the simplest ones. Take the hynid thread and the needle and your four pieces of flesh and sew them together. That should get you what you need. Once you've sewn the clue back together, give it to me, and I may finally be able to understand it.'
_This part of the quest requires a Tailor capable of crafting an item with a 202 and 268 skill check in Tailoring._
_First, you need a Muramite Needle (202 Tailoring):_
\- 4x Bone Chips + 1x Muramite Residue
_By now, you know "Bone Chips" are obviously a common drop from skeletons just about everywhere. They also drop from hynids and cragbeasts in Kod'Taz. If the Seeds of Destruction expansion is open on your server, you can find them in unlimited supply from Apothecary Cadmael [npc=31260] in Oceangreen Village._
_"Muramite Residue" is an uncommon drop from any muramite mobs (kyvs; ra'tuks; ukuns; ikaavs; etc.) in Kod'Taz._
_Next, you'll need a Hynid-Hair Thread (268 Tailoring):_
\- 4x Hynid Hair Strand + 1x Muramite Needle
_"Hynid Hair Strand" is a common drop from hynids in Kod'Taz. The Muramite Needle is always returned to you (success or fail)._
_Finally, you need to create a "Sewn Flesh Parchment" (No-Fail Tailoring):_
\- 4x "Frayed Flesh Scrap" + Muramite Needle + Hynid-Hair Thread
_Hand in the Sewn Flesh Parchment to Tublik Narwethar:_
Tublik Narwethar says 'Nicely sewn, \_\_\_\_\_. I would have tried doing it myself, but I have the most unsteady fingers when it comes to things like that. In any case, let me have a look at what this says.' Tublik looks over the clue for a time before continuing. 'Unbelievable. Simply astounding. These notes weren't made by the Muramites at all. In fact, they were created by trusik priests from their own flesh in some kind of [ritual].'

---

**Additional Clues**
You say, 'What about this ritual?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'I've been assuming that only the Muramites were so cruel that they would rip flesh from another living being in this manner, but it appears that the trusik priests were the ones that did it. They etched glyphs into the skin and then removed it to be used later. The glyphs represent the four temples and their direct connection to the Altar of Destruction. It looks as though there may be [more clues] about the Altar of Destruction and its surrounding temples in the trial temples.'
You say, 'More clues? You don't have enough already?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'I need you to return to the three temples where you participated in the three trials. Based on this clue, I believe you will find three additional clues about the Altar of Destruction in the Temple of Singular Might, the Temple of Twin Struggles, and the Temple of the Tri-Fates. I doubt they'll be hard to find, but you must go to each and search for the clues! Return to me when you have found them!'
_Here, you need 3x "Glyphed Flesh" (three types - ground spawns in each of the three Ikkinz Group Trial temples. Locations for your "kodtaz.txt" map file:_
P 952.1369, 1308.9993, -432.4316, 0, 127, 0, 1, Glyphed\_Flesh
P 288.6153, 1434.7100, -340.4208, 0, 127, 0, 1, Glyphed\_Flesh
P 894.2471, 1626.0768, -402.3225, 0, 127, 0, 1, Glyphed\_Flesh
_In-game /locations:_
Ikkinz Group 1: -1428, -308, -336 (at the very top level of the south rampart, out in the open)
Ikkinz Group 2: -1642, -902, -404 (inside sitting in a bowl on a table)
Ikkinz Group 3: -1045, -704, -407 (next to a bench in the corner, inside)
Tublik stares at the three clues for a moment before speaking. 'I can't be sure, but I believe we are getting close to something big, \_\_\_\_\_. I need more time to examine these clues, but I should have something for you when you return. You've done well and I know that we wouldn't have been able to get this far without your help. Good job!'
You say, 'Give me a progress update'

---

**Three Sanctuaries**
You say, 'Hail, Tublik Narwethar'
Tublik Narwethar says 'I wasn't expecting you back so soon, \_\_\_\_\_, but I'm glad you're here. I've been analyzing the glyphs on the last three artifacts you recovered and, the way I read them, it seems the trusik priests used the [four temples] around the Altar of Destruction to gain access to a temple to face some kind of final rite of passage.'
You say, 'Tell me about the four temples'
Tublik Narwethar says 'As you've already uncovered, the Sanctuary of Divine Destruction was used to uncover the purpose for the other three. I believe the Muramites destroyed that temple because it holds the key to the remaining three temples and the artifacts that they hold. Now that we know what's ahead, we can continue forth and unravel this [mystery].'
You say, 'What about this mystery?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'It's clear to me that you must proceed into each of the three remaining temples and recover the three artifacts that are in them. It's up to you how you want to proceed, but you will have to enter the [Sanctuary of the Righteous], the [Sanctuary of the Glorified], or the [Sanctuary of the Transcendent]. Each one of these holds an artifact that I believe will be used to gain entrance to whatever lies beyond the Altar of Destruction. Which will you choose?'

---

!

---

**Ikkinz, Sanctuary of the Righteous**
_Say "Sanctuary of the Righteous" to request the expedition (requires minimum of six players in /raid format)._
_Complete the raid "Ikkinz, Chambers of Righteousness" (see **this quest entry** [quest=3461] for details) and loot the "Artifact of Righteousness" from the pile of bones that spawns upon its completion._
_Turn in the Artifact to Tublik Narwethar:_
Tublik Narwethar says 'You've done it! You've recovered the first of the three artifacts! You must keep this with you, as you'll need it once you've recovered all three artifacts. You only need two more -- one from the [Sanctuary of the Glorified], and one from the [Sanctuary of the Transcendent]. Which do you think you'd like to do next?'

---

**Ikkinz, Sanctuary of the Glorified**
_Say "Sanctuary of the Glorified" to request the expedition (requires minimum of six players in /raid format)._
_Complete the raid "Ikkinz, Chambers of Glorification" (see **this quest entry** [quest=3462] for details) and loot the "Artifact of Glorification" from the pile of bones that spawns upon its completion._
_Turn in the Artifact to Tublik Narwethar:_
Tublik Narwethar says 'You've done it! You've recovered the second of the three artifacts! You must keep this with you, because you'll need it once you've recovered all three artifacts. You only need one more from the [Sanctuary of the Transcendent]. Are you ready to progress forward with the final artifact?'

---

**Ikkinz, Sanctuary of the Transcendent**
_Say "Sanctuary of the Transcendent" to request the expedition (requires minimum of six players in /raid format)._
_Complete the raid "Ikkinz, Chambers of Transcendence" (see **this quest entry** [quest=3463] for details) and loot the "Artifact of the Righteous" from the pile of bones that spawns upon its completion._
_Turn in the Artifact to Tublik Narwethar:_
Tublik Narwethar says 'You've done it! You've recovered all three of the artifacts! You must keep this with you, because you'll need it now. Somewhere deep down I knew you would be able to get them all. I've been studying the markings from the artifacts and I've come to the conclusion that they must be combined to form a key to enter the [Altar of Destruction].'

---

**Icon of the Altar**
You say, 'What's this Altar of Destruction?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'The Altar of Destruction is far more remarkable than any other temple built in this region. I have been catching up on the history of those three artifacts and now you must harness the power that the geomantic priests once held. That power will combine the three pieces you collected into one final [Icon of the Altar].'
You say, 'An Icon of the Altar?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'From what we've seen of the glyphs in the area, we believe the trusik would use their geomantic powers to meld the three pieces together and form the icon. Since we don't have that luxury, we're going to have to do it another way. I've devised a [plan] that should replicate what they were trying to do, but it will require more effort on your part.'
You say, 'What is the plan?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'The first thing you're going to need is something to meld the three pieces together. I believe that using some inanimate ore from the stone guardians that protect the temples will be perfect for this job. The next thing you'll need is to make an inferno scepter to heat up the ore. You'll have to make the scepter yourself, which can be done by combining a flarestone, flaring coals, an inferno scepter mold and a blazing torch in a forge. Hmm. I believe you'll have to head back to the camp to find some of those things. In any case, there's one [final step] that you need to take before you're done.'
You say, 'What final step?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'Each inferno scepter you make can be used to smelt one block of inanimate ore. Do this three times and you'll get three vials of smelted molten ore. Combine the three vials and the three artifacts in a forge to fuse the three pieces together and hopefully create the Icon of the Altar. When you've finally accomplished all this, show me the icon so I can identify whether this method is one that works or not. Good luck, \_\_\_\_\_.'

---

**Smithing Combines**
_Here, you have some blacksmithing combines to do (any standard Forge)._
Inferno Scepters
_You'll need to make 3x "Inferno Scepter". The recipe for this item is 250 Smithing Trivial and consists of four vendorbought items (receipe produces 1x "Inferno Scepter", so repeat for three combines):_
\- 1x Blazing Torch
\- 1x Flarestone
\- 1x Flaring Coals
\- 1x Inferno Scepter Mold
_Each of these items can be bought from Joran Mullven ( **click for map image**) in the Abysmal Sea. He is found on the topside of the ship in the northwestern part of the zone at about /loc 555, 210, 86._
_Bring lots of platinum with you as these are 2,626 platinum per combine and can be failed. NOTE: As Inferno Scepters are tradeable, you can get someone else to do the combines for you._
_From here, you'll need to make 3x "Vial of Smelted Molten Ore" (no-fail Smithing combine):_
\- 1x "Inanimate Ore" (dropped by golem mobs in Kod'Taz)
\- 1x "Inferno Scepter" (combined in previous step)
_Now, do one final Smithing combine (no-fail):_
\- 3x "Vial of Smelted Molten Ore"
\- 1x "Artifact of Righteousness"
\- 1x "Artifact of Glorification"
\- 1x "Artifact of Transcendence"
_This produces 1x "Icon of the Altar"._

---

**A Final Turn-In**
You say, 'Hail, Tublik Narwethar'
Tublik Narwethar says 'Welcome back, \_\_\_\_\_. It doesn't look like you've been able to finish your Icon of the Altar. Do you remember the [plan] I devised to create it? If not, I can explain it to you once more.'
_Hand him the "Icon of the Altar":_
Tublik scans the icon before returning it to you. 'You've done well, \_\_\_\_\_. Up until now I hadn't noticed the final glyph that is created when the three pieces are combined. I must inspect this further, but return to me shortly and I should have an answer as to the glyph's meaning.'
_You receive it back._
You say, 'Give me a progress update'
Finished! - You can now retry any of the trials at any time!
Finished! - You have completed the trial at the Temple of Singular Might!
Finished! - You have completed the trial at the Temple of Twin Struggles!
Finished! - You have completed the trial at the Temple of the Tri-Fates!
Finished! - You've been commissioned to investigate the Martyrs Passage!
Finished! - You've returned four relics from the Martyrs Passage!
Finished! - You've returned valuable information as to why the Muramites are in the Martyrs Passage!
Finished! - You've been commissioned to investigate the Temple of the Damned!
Finished! - You've recovered important glyphs from the Temple of the Damned!
Finished! - You've successfully translated the glyphs you found in the Temple of the Damned!
Finished! - You've collected the Minor Relics of Power from the Pit of the Lost!
Finished! - You've rescued the artifact from the Ageless Relic Protector in the Pit of the Lost!
Finished! - You've been sent to gather any clues you can find from the Crumbled Sanctuary of Divine Destruction!
Finished! - You have collected the four Frayed Flesh Scraps from the Crumbled Sanctuary of Divine Destruction!
Finished! - You've sewn the flesh scraps together to make the Sewn Flesh Parchment!
Finished! - You've found the three clues from the three trial temples!
Finished! - You've recovered the Artifact of Righteousness!
Finished! - You've recovered the Artifact of Glorification!
Finished! - You've recovered the Artifact of Transcendence!
Finished! - You've been instructed what needs to be done to make the Icon of the Altar!
Finished! - You've constructed your Icon of the Altar!
Kod'Taz still has secrets left to uncover...

---

**Moving On**
_This finishes the preliminary raids for Kod'Taz and Ikkinz._
_You can now request and complete the **final Ikkinz raid** [quest=7598] and get your hands on a Sliver of the High Temple._
- Icon of the Altar [item=30451]

Modified: Mon Jul 13 23:06:23 2026 | | **_Note: If you're Level 62+, you have access to Qvic by default. To gain official access (or enter it under level), all you need is a "Sliver of the High Temple" from the fourth Ikkinz raid (and a turn-in) and a "Fragment of the High Temple" from the Uqua raid. You don't need any of the progression leading up to requesting those raids; you only need those two specific items._**
_This part of Gates of Discord progression follows your **construction of an Icon of the Altar** [quest=7597] (note that some elements of progression can be completed outside of the order outlined here, which is but one path you can take)._

---

!!

---

**Sliver of the High Temple**
You say, 'Hail, Tublik Narwethar'
Tublik Narwethar says 'There you are! I've been waiting for you. I've examined the final glyph on the Icon of the Altar and it seems to suggest that there must have been a final battle between the trusik priests and a keeper of some sort. I can't quite determine what the keeper is, but I imagine it won't be friendly. Before you enter the keeper's inner chambers, you'll need to present your Icon of the Altar to a stone [sentinel] standing guard outside. By all accounts, this seems to be the same path that the priests had to take as well.'
You say, 'What about this sentinel?'
Tublik Narwethar says 'Like the keeper, I don't know what you should expect with the sentinel, so be prepared for anything. I must send you on your way now and wish you whatever luck a poor dwarf can muster. Fortune has treated you well so far... let's hope it continues.'
_Head to the top of the giant temple in the zone's northwest and see the "Sentinel of the Altar" (/loc 620, 1600, -330). Hand it the Icon of the Altar to initiate the final Ikkinz raid expedition._
_Complete the raid expedition **Ikkinz, Antechamber of Destruction** [quest=3464] and loot the "Sliver of the High Temple" from the pile of bones that spawns after the Keeper of the Altar's death._
_Hand in the "Sliver of the High Temple" to Tublik Narwethar:_
Tublik Narwethar says 'You've exceed all my expectations. This key is only the first of what looks to be three needed... needed for what I wonder? I imagine you'll find out more as you continue your adventures. Seek out Brevik in the Abysmal Sea should you discover anything else. He is one of the Wayfarers Brotherhood's resident geomancer adepts. He's not yet fully skilled, but he has much in the way of knowledge of the craft already and learning more each day. He will no doubt have more information for you. For now, I bid you good luck and farewell, \_\_\_\_\_!'
_You receive back a different version of the "Sliver of the High Temple"._

---

**Fragment of the High Temple**
_To obtain a "Fragment of the High Temple", all you need to do is complete the raid expedition " **Uqua, the Ocean God Chantry** [quest=4479]" (see link for details), and hail the "Specter of Barxt" that spawns at the end._
_The Uqua raid is given out by Jerimo Jiao in the southwestern part of Yxtta, the Pulpit of Exiles._

---

**Entering Qvic, Prayer Grounds of Calling**
_Once you've obtained **both** a " **Sliver of the High Temple** [item=30339]" and a **Fragment of the High Temple** [item=28316], have these items in your inventory while you zone into Qvic via Kod'Taz or Yxtta. Upon zoning in, you will be /keyed for the zone._
_From here, you can speak with Madronoa (far northwestern corner of Natimbi) to zone into Qvic:_
You say, 'Hail, Madronoa'
Madronoa says 'Hello \_\_\_\_\_, how are you today? My name is Madronoa and I have recently returned from the Prayer Grounds of Calling. A group of young adventurers such as yourself led me there some time ago. While there I learned of the geomantic barrier that prevents others from entering and I've learned how to channel the energy of that barrier to teleport those such as yourself there. However, you must have acquired the Fragment and Sliver of the High Temple to be able to get past the unseen force preventing adventurers from entering. If you have both of these pieces and to travel to Qvic, I shall create a portal for you.'
You say, 'I wish to travel to Qvic.'
Madronoa says 'Very well, \_\_\_\_\_. Safe travels to you.'
A flash of light occurs as you are teleported away from this place.
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Qvic, Prayer Grounds of Calling.
The magical barrier protecting Qvic softens as you pass through it.

---

**Moving On**
_You can now move onto the next part of Gates of Discord progression: **Access to Txevu** [quest=2917]._

---

**_Note: If all you're interested in is participating in Txevu and Tacvi raids, then you don't need to worry about this quest. If you want the keys to the locked doors within Txevu and want to be able to request the Tacvi raid, then this quest is for you._**
_This part of Gates of Discord progression follows your **having accessed Txevu** [quest=2917]._

---

!

---

**Hamari Nedu**
_At the zone-in of Txevu is a Nihil named Hamari Nedu:_
You say, 'Hail, Hamari Nedu'
Hamari Nedu says 'Aaaaahh! You frightened me! I've spent days hiding in the rubble here waiting for someone to come. Thank Trushar it's you and not one of those monsters! I didn't know how much longer I could hold out, but I have information that may [help you] rid our island of those vile Muramites and help me get out of this cursed place.'
You say, 'Help me'
Hamari Nedu says 'I know the monster that guards this place. He calls himself [Tkarish]. I was held captive by the two-headed beast. I don't know exactly what fate awaited me, but the cries of my fellow prisoners were enough to know that it wouldn't have been pleasant.'
You say, 'Tkarish?'
Hamari Nedu says 'I believe the full title he demanded his servants use is Zun'Muram Tkarish Zyk. Fortunately, he's as arrogant as he is strong and I was able to slip away when he thought me secure. Unfortunately, he has gained control over the sacred [constructs] in the temple so I haven't been able to leave.'
You say, 'What sacred constructs?'
Hamari Nedu says 'There are special constructs within this temple that allow access to other areas. The one next to us would normally allow me to leave the temple entirely, but it is under the sway of the Muramites. I overheard Tkarish speaking of a Tunat'Muram, a kind of commander as far as I could tell, that resides within the upper levels of the temple only reachable via the constructs. But Tkarish holds a [Signet of Command] which gives him control over the constructs. As long as he has that Signet, the way to the Tunat'Muram is closed to you and the way out is closed to me.'
You say, 'Signet of Command?'
Hamari Nedu says 'If you can wrest the signet from Tkarish's grasp, the constructs will allow you passage to the upper levels of the temple. Should you claim a Signet of Command for yourself, I can use it to activate a nearby construct. From here, the influence of your signet would be stronger than the one Tkarish wields in the chapel above and I should be able to convince the construct to grant you passage to the upper levels of the temple.'
_He speaks of Zun`Muram Tkarish Zyk... To get to him, you'll need to do some keying within the zone first._

---

**Entering the High Priest's Caverns**
_To unlock the door to the High Priest's caverns, you'll need a **Polished Stone Key** [item=32891]._
_Trigger the **Gladiator Pit event** [quest=7681] whose main encounter is **Ixt Hsek Syat** [npc=15525]. She drops one key per kill. (Only one person in a raid needs this key.)_

---

**Entering the Zun`Muram's Chamber**
_To unlock the door to the Zun`Muram's chamber, you'll need a **Jade Inlaid Key** [item=35216]._
_Trigger the **High Priest event** [quest=7682] whose main encounter is **High Priest Nkosi Bakari** [npc=15398]. He drops one key per kill. (Only one person in a raid needs this key, although the more you have, the less burden on the keyholders.)_

---

**Signet of Command & Requesting the Tacvi Raid**
_Complete the **Zun`Muram's raid event** [quest=7683], culminating in the death of **Zun`Muram Tkarish Zyk** [npc=15405], who drops 1x " **Zun'Muram's Signet of Command** [item=33843] per kill._
_Upon the Zun`Muram's death, your raid is automatically assigned the Tacvi raid expedition which you can enter by clicking on the golem statue in his chamber. You don't have do this, though._
_With the Signet of Command on you, speak with Hamari Nedu again, and you can request the **Tacvi raid expedition** [quest=3035] at any time._

---

**Finished**
_Now, you're done with Gates of Discord progression. All that is left is the final raid zone of the expansion, Tacvi (and lots of loot to be had)._

---
